/

XNet3L Structure Details:
XNet3L(
  (net_body): ModuleList(
    (0): RGCNConv(16, 64, num_relations=4)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): RGCNConv(64, 64, num_relations=4)
    (3): ReLU()
    (4): RGCNConv(64, 64, num_relations=4)
    (5): ReLU()
  )
  (policy_head): ModuleList(
    (0): Linear(in_features=64, out_features=64, bias=True)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): Linear(in_features=64, out_features=4, bias=True)
  )
  (double_head): ModuleList(
    (0): Linear(in_features=64, out_features=64, bias=True)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): Linear(in_features=64, out_features=1, bias=True)
  )
  (value_head): ModuleList(
    (0): Linear(in_features=192, out_features=64, bias=True)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): Linear(in_features=64, out_features=1, bias=True)
    (3): Tanh()
  )
)
